Hai! ^w^

I'm not the best developer, but I enjoy writing documentation and I= want to learn more programming languages. At the moment I'm trying to get = better at HTML/CSS/JavaScript so I can improve upon this website and create= other websites in the future.

I eventually want to code a Raspberry Pi smart TV. I'm not sure = what to call it yet, so if you have any suggestions or if you would like to= help code it, please tell me :3. I might make it its own Linux distro, alt= hough it's more likely that it will be something like RetroPie but for watching movies and TV.

You can contact me on XMPP<= /a> or send me an email. I ch= eck my email often but not daily, and I only just made this XMPP address re= cently so I may forget to check it, so I apologize if I'm a bit late to get= back to you.

I like Pokemon, EarthBound, Nintendo console modding & homeb= rew, Lucky Star, open-source software, and documentation, I hate AI and lar= ge tech companies (especially Google and Meta), and I love Firefox, Linux, = and my boyfriend >w<

This page's contents are just a slightly modified version of my = GitHub README.md (bio). I might = update it to be more unique, or maybe it will just stay this way.
